Record<number, boolean>,\n    userAutoOptIn: boolean\n  ): ResolvedFeatureState {\n    const globalEnabled = globalEnabledMap.get(featureId) ?? false;\n    const teamStatesById = teamStates,\n      userState,\n      effectiveEnabled,\n      effectiveReason,\n      orgAutoOptIn,\n      teamAutoOptIns,\n      userAutoOptIn,\n    };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * List all opt-in features opt-in features with their raw states for a team or organization.\n   * Used for team/org admin settings page to configure feature opt-in.\n   * Only returns features that are in the allowlist, globally enabled, and scoped to the specified scope.\n   * If parentOrgId is provided, also returns the organization state for each feature.\n   */\n  async listFeaturesForTeam(input: {\n    teamId: `permissive`: User opt-in can activate the feature; any explicit enable above is sufficient;\n *   disables only win if ALL teams disable.\n * - `strict`: User opt-in alone is not enough; requires explicit enable from org/team;\n *   ANY explicit disable blocks.\n */\nexport type OptInFeaturePolicy = \"permissive\" | \"strict\";\n\n/**\n * Scope that determines at which levels a feature can be configured.\n *\n * - `org`: Feature can be configured at the organization level\n * - `team`: Feature can be configured at the team level\n * - `user`: Feature can be configured at the user level\n */\nexport type OptInFeatureScope = \"org\" | \"team\" | \"user\";\n\n/**\n * Normalized feature representation used across all scopes (user, team, org).\n */\nexport interface NormalizedFeature {\n  slug: string;\n  globalEnabled: boolean;\n  /** The current state value for this scope (userState, teamState, etc.) */\n  currentState: FeatureState;\n  /** The reason for the effective state (only available for user scope) */\n  effectiveReason?: EffectiveStateReason;\n}\n\n/**\n * Toggle option labels - differ between user and team/org scopes.\n */\nexport interface ToggleLabels {\n  enabled: string;\n  disabled: string;\n  inherit: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Common interface returned by all feature opt-in hooks.\n * This allows a single component to work with any scope.\n */\nexport interface UseFeatureOptInResult {\n  // Query state\n  features: NormalizedFeature[];\n  autoOptIn: boolean;\n  isLoading: boolean;\n\n  // Mutations\n  setFeatureState: (slug: string, state: FeatureState) => void;\n  setAutoOptIn: (checked: boolean) => void;\n  isStateMutationPending: boolean;\n  isAutoOptInMutationPending: boolean;\n\n  // Scope-specific configuration\n  toggleLabels: ToggleLabels;\n\n  /** Description for the auto opt-in toggle, varies by scope */\n  autoOptInDescription: string;\n\n  /**\n   * For user scope: returns a warning message if the feature is blocked by org/team.\n   * Returns null if not blocked or not applicable (team/org scopes).\n   */\n  getBlockedWarning: (feature: NormalizedFeature) => string | null;\n\n  /**\n   * Returns true if the feature toggle should be disabled because it's blocked by a higher level.\n   * - Here's a summary of what was done:\n\n## Defects Found and Fixed\n\n**File: `packages/trpc/server/routers/viewer/featureOptIn/_router.ts`**\n\n### 3 Functional Bugs (Scope Validation Missing)\n\n`setUserState`, `setTeamState`, and `setOrganizationState` all lacked scope validation at the router layer. When a caller sent a feature slug not available at the relevant scope, the service would throw `ErrorWithCode` — but since `ErrorWithCode` extends `Error` (not `TRPCError`), tRPC maps it to HTTP 500 (INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR) instead of the intended HTTP 400 (BAD_REQUEST).\n\n**Fix:** Added `isFeatureAllowedForScope(input.slug, \"<scope>\")` checks in each mutation before delegating to the service, throwing `TRPCError({ code: \"BAD_REQUEST\" })` on violation.\n\n### 3 Type Compliance Violations (Unconditional `assignedBy`)\n\nAll three mutation endpoints always passed `assignedBy: ctx.user.id` regardless of the state value. The service's `setUserFeatureState` / `setTeamFeatureState` accept discriminated unions where the `\"inherit\"` variant must NOT include `assignedBy` — only the `\"enabled\"` / `\"disabled\"` variants require it.\n\n**Fix:** Split each service call into a conditional branch: the `\"inherit\"` branch omits `assignedBy`, the other branch includes it.\n\n### No Defects in the Other Two Files\n\n- `config.ts`: scope filter logic, `isFeatureAllowedForScope`, and the `HAS_*` constants are all correct.\n- `FeatureOptInService.ts`: scope filtering in `listFeaturesForUser`/`listFeaturesForTeam`, scope validation in the service methods, and discriminated union handling are all correct."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":201,"cache_read_input_tokens":74011,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":201},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"95592dfb-9659-44bf-bb62-51cdb55ee500","uuid":"cefac101-9486-4979-a5c0-d3891eb98c9e"}
